So the flag has a *marketed* meaning but was **never specified to be enforced**, and gating it now would (a) remove a currently-free, load-bearing page from free users, (b) require deciding which analytics are "advanced" vs basic (never defined), and (c) conflict with `improvement_proof` being deliberately used as the FREE paywall-modal conversion hook ("your rewrites gain +X%"). Per the task's own instruction ("if genuinely nothing concrete was ever specified, flag it back rather than guess and implement a gate for an undefined feature"), left UNGATED and flagged for a product decision. If it should be Pro: decide the exact scope first (whole page? just improvement_proof? specific charts?), then add `assertAdvancedAnalytics` + gate `/profile/analytics` and keep the paywall-modal's improvement_proof fetch working (it's the conversion hook).
  - **DELIBERATE FREE-TIER SURFACES — do NOT gate these without an explicit discussion.** The following are free-by-design (the top-of-funnel that makes the product usable and drives trial/Pro conversion); a future session must not "tidy up" a Pro gate onto any of them without a real product conversation:
    - **PYQ papers** (full PYQ bank + explanations + weightage analytics) — free (`featPYQ` is free:true on the pricing page).
    - **Sectional practice** — free.
    - **Custom build-from-bank** (`createCustomTestFromNode`/custom sets over the *existing* published bank) — free. NOTE the distinction: on-demand *generation* of a fresh mock (`createFreshMockSet`, `assertMockTests`) IS Pro; building a set by *selecting* from the already-published bank is free.
    - **Daily quiz** — free.
    - **Current affairs** (feed, detail, per-fact add-to-revision) — free.
    - **Revision / SRS** (FSRS scheduling, review player, manage, seeds) — free.
    - **Community** (discussion, peer review, voting) — free.
    - **Scoreboards / leaderboard** — free.
    - **Magazine WEB VIEW** (both monthly editions, read online in-app) — free. ONLY the PDF export/download is Pro (`magazine_pdf`, see above).
    - Free tier also includes: 3 lifetime AI evaluations, 10 mentor msgs/day, top-5 notes/paper by weightage.
  - **VERIFICATION — trial/subscription-expiry reverts every gate on the very next request, no logout/refresh (tested live against the cloud DB).** Confirmed by code that every gate re-reads plan FRESH per call via `getPlanFor`/`getTrialContext` (a `users_profile` select each time; no per-request/middleware plan cache — the only cache is `listFreeNoteNodeIds`' 60s cache of WHICH notes are free, not the user's plan). Then proved it live: forced `plan_expires_at` into the past mid-session for a throwaway trial-shaped account and, without any re-fetch, re-invoked each gate's exact enforcement function. `getPlanFor`'s lazy downgrade fired immediately (plan→free, row persisted). Results — all 8 flip ALLOW→BLOCK on the next call:

    | # | Feature | Enforcement | Before lapse (trial) | After lapse (free), same call path |
    |---|---------|-------------|----------------------|-------------------------------------|
    | 1 | Evaluations | `assertEvaluationCredit` → `getEvaluationQuota` → `getTrialContext` | ALLOW (2/day, remaining 2) | BLOCK 402 (free 3 lifetime, exhausted) |
    | 2 | Mentor | `getMentorQuota` → `getTrialContext` (`enforceDailyLimit`) | ALLOW (limit 15, remaining 3) | BLOCK (limit 10, remaining 0 → 429) |
    | 3 | OCR (handwritten) | `assertHandwrittenOcr` → `assertPro` → `getPlanFor` | ALLOW | BLOCK 402 |
    | 4 | Micro-drills | `assertMicroDrill` → `assertPro` → `getPlanFor` | ALLOW | BLOCK 402 |
    | 5 | Mocks | `assertMockTests` → `assertPro` → `getPlanFor` | ALLOW | BLOCK 402 |
    | 6 | Full notes | `canReadFullNote` → `getPlanFor` | ALLOW (gated node readable) | BLOCK (false for non-free node) |
    | 7 | On-demand generation | `createFreshMockSet` → `assertMockTests` → `getPlanFor` | ALLOW | BLOCK 402 |
    | + | Magazine PDF (new) | `assertMagazinePdf` → `assertPro` → `getPlanFor` | ALLOW | BLOCK 402 `magazine_pdf` |

    Also validated at the real HTTP boundary for the new magazine endpoints (same access token, no refresh): trial/Pro → 200, lapsed→free → 402 `feature=magazine_pdf`, web-view → 200 throughout. All throwaway users + seeded rows deleted by explicit id afterward. `pnpm --filter api typecheck` + `pnpm --filter web build` clean.

## Guest/anonymous browsing via Supabase native anonymous auth (2026-07-28)
A newcomer's first, session-less visit now silently gets a real anonymous Supabase session and lands in the actual app (not a static marketing page), can use every genuinely-free-to-serve surface, and is prompted to sign up (framed as "create an account + your 7-day trial", never a generic paywall) the moment they reach an AI/paid surface — with the anonymous→real upgrade preserving all their progress in place. Built on Supabase's NATIVE anonymous auth (`signInAnonymously`), not a separate demo-data system: a guest is just an `authenticated`-role user with `is_anonymous:true`, so the entire existing stack (JWT verify, RLS, per-user API scoping) treats them like any user with zero special-casing.
  - **THE CRITICAL FIX — migration `0104_anonymous_guest_auth.sql`.** `handle_new_user()` (0052, +0075's trial grant) fires on EVERY `auth.users` INSERT, so a native anonymous signup would have unconditionally consumed a 7-day Pro trial. Rewrote the trigger to branch on `new.is_anonymous`: anonymous → a plain FREE profile (column defaults, `has_used_trial` stays false, no expiry); real signup → the unchanged 0075 behavior (`plan='pro'`, `now()+7d`, `has_used_trial=true`). VERIFIED at the SQL level in a rolled-back transaction (0 rows persisted): a synthetic `is_anonymous=true` insert → free/no-expiry/no-trial; a synthetic real insert → pro/+7d/trial. Applied to the cloud DB via the IPv6 direct host (see [[supabase-headless-migrations]]).
  - **Trial ONLY at real signup — including the upgrade path.** Converting a guest (email/password `updateUser`, or Google `linkIdentity`) is an UPDATE to the SAME `auth.users` row (`is_anonymous` flips false), which does NOT re-fire the AFTER INSERT trigger. So the trial for a converting guest is granted at conversion time IN CODE: `services/trial.ts::claimTrial(userId)` re-reads the user from the Auth admin API (authoritative, not the possibly-stale client JWT), confirms they're no longer anonymous, and grants the trial scoped `.eq("has_used_trial", false)` (idempotent replay guard — a fresh real signup already has it true and is never re-granted). Exposed as `POST /auth/claim-trial` (authenticated); the web calls it after any conversion, driven by a `neev.pendingTrialClaim` localStorage flag set at conversion start (survives the OAuth redirect) so a never-guest signup makes no redundant call. Net invariant: trial granted exactly once, only at a real signup (fresh OR guest-upgrade), never for a pure guest.
  - **Server-side is_anonymous plumbing + AI gates.** `lib/jwt.ts` `verifyAccessToken` now returns `{userId, isAnonymous}` (from the `is_anonymous` JWT claim; also on the HS256 `getUser` fallback); `lib/user-context.ts` carries it in the async store with a non-throwing `currentUserIsAnonymous()` (false outside a request, so jobs are unaffected — `runWithUser` gained an optional `{isAnonymous}` arg, back-compatible for every existing CLI/scheduler caller). `entitlements.ts` gained `assertNotGuest(feature)` → a 402 flagged `requiresSignup:true` (new `HttpError` option, serialized alongside `feature` in the error envelope; `ApiError` on the web reads it). Wired at the three AI spend points BEFORE any model cost: `evaluate.ts` `createSubmission`/`planEvaluation`/`planOcr` and `mentor/index.ts` `planDoubtMessage`. `getEntitlements` returns a new `is_guest` flag (shared `entitlementsSchema`) so the UI shows "sign up to unlock", never a free-tier "N left" a guest can't spend.
  - **Abuse mitigation — per-IP, separate from per-user (task requirement).** TWO layers: (1) the authoritative, UNBYPASSABLE backstop is Supabase's own GoTrue `anonymous_users` rate limit = **30 sign-ins/hour/IP** (it sees the true client IP because the browser calls `signInAnonymously` directly; config.toml + dashboard). (2) An app-level `POST /auth/guest` checkpoint the web calls BEFORE signing in, a NEW `lib/rate-limit.ts::rateLimitByIp` keyed strictly by `req.ip` (distinct from the per-user `rateLimit`), at **10 guest sessions/hour/IP** — deliberately UNDER GoTrue's 30 so our friendly localized "create a free account" nudge fires before GoTrue's opaque 429. Reasoning: a real visitor needs ~1 guest session/device; 10/hr tolerates modest shared-NAT (hostel/college/library — common for UPPSC aspirants) + cookie-clears while stopping scripted mass-creation; anyone past it is nudged to sign up (a real account isn't IP-throttled the same way) — the exact conversion we want. `app.set("trust proxy", true)` in production so `req.ip` is the real client IP behind Cloudflare/Render (a spoofed XFF only rotates OUR app-level key; GoTrue's real-IP limit still backstops). LIVE-TESTED: `POST /auth/guest` returns 200 then 429 exactly at the 11th call/hour/IP. Same per-instance in-memory caveat as the existing limiter (swap for a shared store on a multi-instance deploy).
  - **RLS — verified, not assumed (task requirement).** A DB-wide `pg_policies` scan confirms **NO policy references `is_anonymous`** anywhere (public + storage), so every owner-scoped (`to authenticated using auth.uid()=user_id`) and content-read (`to anon,authenticated`) policy applies to a guest uniformly — a guest reads/writes only its OWN rows and reads only published content. A dedicated `pnpm --filter api guest:rls` (`scripts/guest-rls-check.ts`) proves it LIVE against the cloud DB using the anon key + a real anonymous JWT (the exact browser credentials): guest profile is free/no-trial (the 0104 trigger firing on a genuine `signInAnonymously`), guest CAN read public content + insert/read its OWN attempt, CANNOT read/insert another user's rows, CANNOT write content tables, and the API `/entitlements` reports `is_guest:true`. It skips gracefully (exit 0) when anonymous sign-ins are disabled, so it never blocks CI before the toggle is on. **LIVE-VERIFIED once the founder enabled the toggle: `guest:rls` = 10/10 PASS** against the cloud DB with a real anonymous JWT.
  - **FULL LIVE BROWSER E2E — 19/19 PASS** (`scratchpad guest-e2e.mjs`, Playwright, temp): a real `navigator.webdriver=true` visit does NOT auto-create a guest (prerender-safe) and stays on the landing; a disguised (webdriver=false) first visit auto-creates a guest session and lands on the dashboard with the same-device banner; the guest completes a REAL daily-quiz attempt through the API with its own token; `/entitlements` reports `is_guest`/free/no-trial; the server blocks a guest evaluation with 402 `requiresSignup` AND the eval quota chip shows "sign up to unlock" whose click opens the SIGNUP modal (not the Pro paywall); the guest banner renders in Hindi at 390px with zero console errors; and the full conversion invariant holds — converting in place keeps the SAME auth row, the guest's daily-quiz attempt + profile survive under that same id, no trial exists until claimed, `POST /auth/claim-trial` then grants the 7-day trial (pro + expiry) and is idempotent on a second call. Every synthetic user (guest + throwaway B + converted) deleted by explicit id afterward — 0 leftover anonymous/test users confirmed. (Note: the live email→real conversion in the E2E is completed via the admin API because the cloud project has `mailer_autoconfirm:false`; the browser `updateUser` path is real but needs the emailed link — Google `linkIdentity` remains the smoother path and needs Manual Linking on.)
  - **Web — auto-guest, gating, conversion.** `AuthProvider` gained `isGuest` (`user.is_anonymous`), `signInAnonymously` (via the `/auth/guest` gate), `linkGoogle`/`convertGuestWithPassword` (data-preserving conversion, both set the pending-trial-claim flag). `components/marketing/guest-autostart.tsx` (mounted on the landing route) silently creates a guest session on a first session-less visit and navigates to the dashboard — SKIPS bots/prerender (`navigator.webdriver`, so crawlers still get the prerendered marketing HTML and the build's prerender pass creates no junk rows), one attempt per tab, and FAILS OPEN (anonymous disabled/rate-limited/offline → the landing page just stays, i.e. shipping this before the toggle is on is a no-op). `RequireAuth` lets a guest skip the onboarding+welcome gates (those are for a real account; a guest hits them after converting) and mounts `useClaimTrialOnConversion`. Gating UI: `PaywallModal` renders a "create your free account + 7-day trial" variant whenever `isGuest` (so EVERY 402 trigger is guest-aware), plus proactive `isGuest` checks at the eval/OCR (`answers-write`) and mentor (`mentor-chat`) entry points open it instantly with no wasted request; the eval quota chip shows a signup state for guests. Same-device honesty (task requirement): a persistent `GuestBanner` on every app-shell page ("progress is saved on this device — create a free account to keep it + start your trial") and a guest-specific account menu (Create free account / same-device note / Exit). `auth.tsx` detects a guest and switches Google→`linkIdentity` and sign-up→`updateUser`, with a "your progress comes with you" banner. Bilingual copy added to `messages/{en,hi}.json` (`Auth.guest*`, `Guest.*`) + `billing-copy.ts`.
  - **Two required dashboard toggles (documented in TODO above, can't be set via API here — no management token):** Anonymous Sign-Ins (project-level) and, for Google guest-conversion, Manual Linking. Also found `mailer_autoconfirm:false` on the cloud project → a guest converting via EMAIL must click the emailed confirmation link (built-in email is rate-limited), so **Google is the smoother guest-conversion path**; the email path's `needsConfirmation` branch is handled honestly in the UI.
  - **Offline verification (all clean):** `pnpm --filter api typecheck`, web `tsc -b` + full `vite build`, `oxlint` (no new warnings); migration applied + trigger branch proven via rolled-back SQL; RLS `is_anonymous` grep; `POST /auth/guest` 200→429 rate-limit + `claim-trial` 401-without-auth tested against the running API.
  - **FOLLOW-UP (same day, from founder feedback on the live UI):** two fixes. (1) **Visible guest entry** — "no option on the UI" (the silent auto-guest is a no-op while the toggle is off, so a visitor saw no way in): added an explicit **"Explore as a guest"** button (`components/marketing/guest-entry-button.tsx`) to the landing hero AND the auth page (`Guest.exploreCta`/`unavailable`, en+hi). It calls `signInAnonymously` → dashboard, and degrades HONESTLY when anonymous is disabled/rate-limited (an inline "guest mode isn't available — sign in" note, not a dead button) — so it works the moment the toggle flips and never looks broken before then. (2) **Google login "had to do it twice"** — root-caused to a PRE-EXISTING race in `auth-callback.tsx` (NOT the guest work: empirically confirmed a failed `signInAnonymously` writes no PKCE code-verifier, so it can't clobber a subsequent OAuth). The callback declared failure the instant it saw no `?code=` + no session, but supabase-js's `detectSessionInUrl` exchange is async and can strip `?code=` a tick before the session propagates through the provider — so the first attempt bounced to sign-in and the user retried. Fixed: wait a grace window (8s, matching the `auth-reset.tsx` precedent) for the session before failing, and carry the reason to `/auth?error=` (surfaced in the auth form) instead of a silent bounce. Verified in-browser (both locales, 390px): guest button visible + graceful-degradation note on a toggle-off click, callback no longer instant-bounces, no horizontal overflow; web `tsc -b` + `vite build` clean. (The one console line in the check was a benign `429` from the localhost per-IP `/auth/guest` bucket being exhausted by this session's own repeated testing — the button still degraded correctly.)
  - **EDGE-CASE AUDIT (post-live-verification) — 2 real gaps found + fixed, both a guest writing to a SOCIAL/COMPETITIVE surface that "guests are read-only there" implied but nothing enforced:**
    - **Community was fully writable by guests.** The 9 community write endpoints (createThread/addPost/votePost/shareAnswerForPeerReview/reportContent/blockUser/…) had NO guest gate — a guest could post/vote/report/share, contradicting the documented "community read-only for guests" decision AND opening an anonymous-spam / low-accountability-moderation hole. Fixed: `assertNotGuest("community", …)` at the top of every community participation write in `services/community.ts` (a 402 `requiresSignup` with a "create a free account to join the discussion" message), plus proactive `isGuest`→`openPaywall` guards on the reply composer, new-thread submit, and vote button so a guest gets the instant signup modal instead of a submit-then-error. LIVE-VERIFIED: a guest token creating a thread (valid payload) → 402 `requiresSignup=true`; `report` → 402.
    - **Guest daily-quiz results were landing on the public leaderboard.** `recordDailyQuizResult` (run at attempt-submit) upserts `daily_quiz_board_entries` for ANY user, so a guest appeared on the competitive daily board `handle:null`. Fixed with an early `if (currentUserIsAnonymous()) return` — the guest's attempt is still scored/saved, just never placed on the board (a converted account starts appearing on the next day's quiz). Only caller is the submit request path (verified), so the context read is correct. LIVE-VERIFIED: a guest daily-quiz submit → **0** `daily_quiz_board_entries` rows.
    - **Confirmed NOT bugs (checked, so they aren't re-investigated):** the Pro-feature gates (mocks/micro-drills/all-notes/magazine-pdf) return a plain 402 that the **isGuest-aware `PaywallModal` already renders as the signup prompt**, so a guest hitting them is funneled to signup with no server change needed; the mentor **autoSend** effect sets its ref BEFORE calling the (guest-gated) `sendMessage`, so it can't loop and the guest gate fires; `getEntitlements`/`assertNotGuest`/`currentUserIsAnonymous` all return safe defaults outside a request context (jobs unaffected).
    - **The two items originally flagged here are now BOTH FIXED (see the "Guest edge-case follow-ups" note below):** (1) `v_test_leaderboard`/`mv_test_leaderboard` now excludes anonymous users (migration 0105); (2) abandoned guests are pruned by a nightly retention job (`guests:prune`).
    - Verified: `pnpm --filter api typecheck`, web `tsc -b` + `vite build`, `oxlint` all clean; both new gates live-tested; every synthetic guest deleted by explicit id (0 leftover anonymous users).
  - **GUEST EDGE-CASE FOLLOW-UPS (the two items flagged above, now BOTH fixed):**
    - **Guests off the per-test/sectional leaderboards — migration `0105_exclude_guests_from_leaderboards.sql`.** `mv_test_leaderboard` wraps the base view `v_test_leaderboard` (mock + SECTIONAL tests; sectional practice is FREE, so a guest could appear there). `create or replace view` adds `and not exists (select 1 from auth.users u where u.id = a.user_id and u.is_anonymous)` to the qualifying CTE (column list unchanged, so the dependent mv stays valid), then refreshes `mv_test_leaderboard` + `mv_mock_series_board`. The view reads `auth.users` as its owner (default `security_invoker=false`), which has the access. Applied to the cloud DB (IPv6 direct); verified `pg_get_viewdef` now contains the filter. (The daily-quiz board was already write-path-guarded; the mains boards require evaluations guests can't make.)
    - **Abandoned-guest retention — `services/guest-cleanup.ts` `pruneAbandonedGuests` + `pnpm guests:prune` (dry-run default, `--apply`, `--days N`), run nightly by `nightly:settle`.** Prunes an anonymous user only when it's BOTH created > `GUEST_RETENTION_DAYS` (env, default 30) ago AND has NO real interaction in that window — no `events` row and no `attempts` row since the cutoff. **Deliberately uses raw `events`/`attempts`, NOT `users_profile.last_active_date`** (which is only set on a study-DAY activity — a guest who merely browses chapters/CA fires events but no study-day, and must not be pruned as abandoned; the first test cut used `last_active_date` and would have wrongly pruned a browsing guest). The activity queries use `selectAll` (cap-safe paging) so an active guest's id can never be truncated out of the keep-set. Deletion order: `users_profile` first (children `attempts`/`events`/… are `ON DELETE CASCADE` from it — verified via FK introspection; `users_profile.id` has NO FK to `auth.users`, confirming the delete-both gotcha), then the auth user; each delete re-confirms `is_anonymous` so a guest who converts between scan and delete is never removed. **Converted and real accounts are never candidates** (they aren't `is_anonymous`). LIVE-VERIFIED with backdated (40-day-old) synthetic guests: one with no activity → pruned (auth + profile + cascaded events gone); one with a recent `events` row → correctly KEPT; a converted account is never listed. Wired into `nightly-settle.ts` as a non-fatal global step (a prune failure never blocks the streak/mastery settle); `GUEST_RETENTION_DAYS` documented in `.env.example`.
    - Verified: `pnpm --filter api typecheck` clean; migration applied + view filter confirmed live; prune dry-run + `--apply` behavior proven against the cloud DB with synthetic backdated guests; 0 leftover anonymous/test users.
  - **SECOND EDGE-CASE PASS (audit of the leaderboard + prune changes themselves) — found + fixed 2 more:**
    - **The prune's activity signal was TOO NARROW.** First cut kept a guest "active" via `events` OR `attempts`, but `events` is fired from ONLY the learn/notes pages (`note_read`/`note_section_read`/`syllabus_node_view` — verified by grepping every `record`/`name:` call site); a guest active via REVISION (srs) or PERSONAL NOTES fires no event and (for revision) no attempt, so they'd have been wrongly pruned. Broadened the keep-signal to a durable-activity row in the window across ALL free-guest surfaces: `events` + `attempts` + `srs_reviews` + `srs_cards` + `user_notes` (all confirmed to have `user_id`+`created_at`). `.in()` chunk dropped to 100 ids (URL-length safety per the repo's `.in()` gotcha). LIVE-VERIFIED with 3 backdated guests: abandoned → pruned; revision-active (recent `srs_cards`) → KEPT; notes-active (recent `user_notes`) → KEPT.
    - **`question_reports` writes weren't guest-gated** — a guest could spam the PYQ-error moderation queue (an anonymous, unaccountable flag entering an admin queue — the same category as the community `reports` I gated in the first pass). Added `assertNotGuest("question_report")` in `createQuestionReport` + a proactive `isGuest`→`openPaywall` guard on `ReportQuestionSheet`. LIVE-VERIFIED: guest → 402 `requiresSignup`. (Personal, own-data writes — attempts, revision, notes — stay FREE for guests; only shared/moderation-queue writes are gated.)
    - **Confirmed NOT problems (checked against the live schema):** pruning a guest never hits a blocking FK — the only 4 non-CASCADE FKs referencing `users_profile` are `SET NULL` (`llm_calls.user_id`, `question_reports.user_id`/`resolved_by`, `reports.resolved_by`), so the profile delete succeeds and ~30 child tables cascade cleanly (no orphans); and the nightly `refresh_scoreboard_views` RPC still succeeds now that `v_test_leaderboard` references `auth.users` (the view reads it as its owner). Both verified by direct SQL.
    - Verified: `pnpm --filter api typecheck`, web `tsc -b` + `vite build`, `oxlint` all clean; both new gates live-tested; 0 leftover anonymous/test users.

## PRODUCTION OUTAGE (2026-07-28): Cloudflare edge-cache poisoning of `/assets/*` — NOT a code bug
The whole app crashed to the error boundary on every load at neevstudy.com (confirmed in a private window). **The reported root cause — "`c05e6c5` deleted `apps/web/src/lib/guest.ts` while `auth-provider.tsx`/`use-claim-trial.ts` still import from it" — was WRONG, and checking it first is what kept this from becoming a wasted fix.** Recorded in full because the real failure mode is invisible to every local check and will recur on the next deploy.
  - **`guest.ts` was never deleted.** `c05e6c5` removed only the two now-unused *autostart* helpers (`guestAutostartAlreadyTried`/`markGuestAutostartTried`) from the file and KEPT `markTrialClaimPending`/`hasTrialClaimPending`/`clearTrialClaimPending` (`git show c05e6c5 -- apps/web/src/lib/guest.ts` shows a 23-line deletion, not a file delete). The file is tracked and present at HEAD; both imports resolve; `pnpm --filter web build` passes; the built `dist/` loads in a real browser with **zero** page errors. Nothing was restored or relocated, because nothing was missing — the only change this incident made to `apps/web` was none at all.
  - **REAL ROOT CAUSE — Cloudflare's edge cache was serving the SPA-fallback `index.html` under `/assets/<chunk>.js` URLs.** The browser rejects it (`Expected a JavaScript-or-Wasm module script but the server responded with a MIME type of "text/html"`), React Router's lazy import throws `Failed to fetch dynamically imported module: .../assets/locale-layout-DqGlLNVJ.js`, and that propagates to the route error boundary → "Something went wrong" on every load. Reproduced exactly with headless Chromium against the live site.
  - **How it was proven** (the diagnostic that matters — a naive status-code sweep MISSES this entirely): Cloudflare's SPA fallback answers a missing asset with **HTTP 200**, not 404, so `curl -sI` over all 62 referenced chunks reported "0 failures" while the app was fully down. The tell is **content-type / body**, not status. Sweeping by content-type found 3-4 poisoned chunks; the decisive observation was `revision-De6_QtiV.js` returning `text/html` on `cf-cache-status: HIT` but `application/javascript` on `MISS` — i.e. **the origin has the file, the edge cache does not**. Re-fetching every chunk with a `?cb=<random>` cache-buster returned real JS for ALL of them, confirming the deployment is complete and healthy and the poisoning is purely per-edge-node (which is why it looked intermittent, and why different users/locations saw different breakage).
  - **Why it is PERMANENT rather than self-healing:** `apps/web/public/_headers` stamps `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` on `/assets/*` **by path, regardless of what was actually served** — so the moment a fallback-HTML body is returned at an asset URL, the edge caches that HTML for a year. Mechanism: during a deploy rollout some edges still served the previous deployment (which lacks the new build's chunk hashes) → SPA fallback → 200 HTML → cached immutable → the new deployment goes fully live but those edges keep serving the year-cached HTML.
  - **Why a private window did NOT help** (and why the user's test was still valid evidence): incognito bypasses the *browser* cache, never the *CDN* cache. Same for a hard refresh.
  - **Why `fd603b8`'s `vite:preloadError` handler didn't save it:** it reloads once per 10s to fetch a fresh `index.html` — but the fresh HTML references the SAME poisoned chunk URL, so the reload fails identically and (by design) the second failure inside the window falls through to the error boundary. That handler fixes *stale-tab* chunk 404s; it cannot fix a poisoned edge entry.
  - **REMEDIATION.** (1) **Purge the Cloudflare cache** for neevstudy.com (dashboard → Caching → Purge Everything, or the API) — the only guaranteed, immediate fix. It is an OWNER action: `wrangler whoami` is unauthenticated in this environment and no `CLOUDFLARE_*`/`CF_*` token exists in any `.env`, so it cannot be done from here. (2) A **redeploy also clears it in practice**, because a fresh build emits different content hashes (verified: local build produced `locale-layout-Drmyz5w4.js`/`revision-1pJSQcy3.js`/`scoreboard-BY1vuZ_Z.js` vs production's `-DqGlLNVJ`/`-De6_QtiV`/`-B2xf0Ojp`), so the new `index.html` points at URLs that were never poisoned.
  - **DURABLE FIX — NOT shipped here, deliberately, and this is the important part.** The correct prevention is to stop `/assets/*` from EVER falling through to the SPA shell (serve a real 404 instead, which is not cacheable-as-immutable and lets the `vite:preloadError` reload actually work). That means editing `_redirects` — the exact file whose last "obvious" fix caused a **real, total production outage** (the `__spa-fallback.html` infinite-redirect incident documented in the TODO section above). Note `_redirects` ALREADY has `/assets/*  /assets/:splat  200` above the wildcard and it did not prevent this. Per that incident's own recorded lesson, a routing change must be validated on a **real Cloudflare preview deploy** against Cloudflare's own clean-URL layer — never shipped blind, and above all not blind *during an active outage*. A lower-risk alternative worth weighing on the preview: drop `immutable` / shorten `max-age` on `/assets/*` so a future poisoning expires in minutes instead of a year (bounds the blast radius rather than preventing it; costs some repeat-visit browser caching). **Do not attempt either straight to production.**
  - **Verification performed:** `pnpm --filter web build` clean; a deliberate `rm apps/web/src/lib/guest.ts` made the build fail cleanly with `TS2307` naming both call sites (proving the build catches this bug class, and that the reported cause never existed), then the file was restored byte-identical (`git status` clean); the built `dist/` served locally and loaded in headless Chromium renders the full landing page in both locales with **0 console errors, 0 failed requests, 0 HTTP >=400**. The new "run a full build before pushing" hard rule is in Dev conventions above.
  - **RESOLVED ~25 min after diagnosis — and the way it resolved CONFIRMS the diagnosis.** Production recovered while still serving the **same, unchanged build** (`index-SaHjWWyd.js` — the pushed redeploy had not yet landed): all 62 chunks re-swept clean (0 serving `text/html`), the 4 known-poisoned chunks returned real JS on 6/6 probes each, and 7 routes (`/en`, `/hi`, `/en/revision`, `/en/scoreboard`, `/en/pricing`, `/en/about`, `/hi/pricing`) all rendered with **0 console errors** in headless Chromium. Zero code changed between broken and working, which is decisive proof this was never a code defect — the poisoned edge entries were simply evicted. **Caveat that matters: eviction is per-edge-node and was observed from ONE location.** Other colos can still hold poisoned entries, so a report of "still down for me" is expected and is NOT a regression — the CDN purge remains the only way to clear it globally and instantly. Practical takeaway for next time: this failure mode *can* self-heal on eviction, so "it started working on its own" is not evidence the cause was found — verify by content-type, not by one page load.

## UPSC GS syllabus tree (2026-07-30): 202 nodes from 75 verbatim official lines, coverage-gated
The complete UPSC Civil Services **General Studies** syllabus tree — hand-authored against the official notification, gated by a bidirectional coverage verifier, embedded, and bound into the exam registry by migration `0112`. **`upsc` stays `is_live: false`**: a syllabus tree is not a launchable exam (no question bank, no chapters, and U6's ~74 unauthored config slots still make every model-facing path throw). Full actionable index in `docs/OUTSTANDING.md` **§8g**; the machine-checkable artifact is `docs/upsc-syllabus-coverage.md`.
  - **WHY A NEW PIPELINE — `ingest:syllabus` is unusable for a second exam, for three independently fatal reasons (all verified; do not re-litigate).** (1) `loadLangSource` hardcodes the manifest ids `uppsc_syllabus_2026_${lang}`/`uppsc_syllabus_drishti_${lang}` — it physically cannot load a UPSC PDF. (2) It **LLM-invents** the tree (`structuredJson`, sonnet, effort high), and a generated tree can never pass a verbatim official-text coverage gate. (3) `buildStructurePaperSystem` and `fillBilingual` both call `requireAuthored(getExamConfig(exam)…)`, which **throws for `upsc` by design** (U6). So: hand-authored data + a deterministic **zero-LLM** loader in `apps/api/src/ingest/seed/` (`pnpm ingest:upsc-syllabus`). No Anthropic call anywhere in that directory. The one real API cost this session was **404 embeddings** (~5 pooled OpenAI calls) — a fraction of a cent.
  - **THE COVERAGE GATE IS THE DELIVERABLE, not the tree.** `verify-coverage.ts` is a pure function (no DB/network/clock/model) implementing **13 defect kinds + 3 info kinds**; the write path refuses on any defect. Shipped result: **0 defects, 75 lines, 195 authored nodes**. Every one of the 13 kinds was fired against synthetic data before being trusted. Two second-order holes closed by design: an **invalid** line reference is excluded from a node's `own` set, so a bad reference cannot make an invented topic *look* covered (and the line it named still reports unmapped); and descendant detection is prefix-based, so one `missing_parent_path` cannot cascade into phantom `invented_topic` reports.
  - **`derivation` — how "no invented topics" was made CHECKABLE rather than aspirational.** UPSC Prelims publishes only **7** official bullets (Paper I) and **6** (Paper II) against UPPSC `PRE_GS1`'s 31 nodes, so "every node title is a literal official phrase" was never achievable at comparable grain. The enforced rule instead: every node traces to ≥1 official line, **and** every node that is not literal official text is declared `editorial_subdivision` + carries a `subdivisionRationale`. Final split **174 `official_line` / 21 `editorial_subdivision`** — far more official than expected, because UPSC's bullets enumerate their own sub-clauses ("Physical, Social, Economic Geography"). **GS-II, GS-IV, CSAT and Essay needed ZERO editorial subdivision**; GS-IV reached 34 nodes from 7 packed bullets on semicolon boundaries alone. Both values persist to `syllabus_nodes.meta.derivation`/`.meta.subdivision_rationale`, so the audit survives in the DB, not just in markdown.
  - **THREE DELIBERATE NON-ADDITIONS** (each is exactly the defect the gate exists to catch): (a) `Science and Technology Developments` under Prelims "General Science." — **UPPSC's own tree has it**, UPSC's text neither names nor implies it; (b) **Essay topic buckets** — UPSC's entire Essay syllabus is ONE instruction paragraph naming ZERO topics, so that paper has **5 nodes** (all `official_line`, built from the assessed dimensions it does name) against UPPSC's 20, with the divergence recorded in an `ambiguityNote` rather than padded away; (c) anything to satisfy the official text's trailing unenumerated `etc.` An authoring agent also self-caught one genuine interpretive step in its own output via a lexical provenance audit and removed it ("Essay Paper: Scope and Topic Choice" → the verbatim "Essays on Multiple Topics", since "choice" was inference).
  - **22 `ambiguityNote`s — FLAGGED, never silently resolved.** The notification **contradicts itself** (`Bio diversity` in the syllabus heading vs `Bio-diversity` in the marks table p.27 — syllabus-heading form used, conflict recorded); `GS2-L03` is genuinely **ungrammatical** in the official text (confirmed across 4 years, not an extraction artefact); `P2-L06` comma-joins "Basic numeracy" and "Data interpretation" in one bullet; `GS1-L02`'s span **wholly contains** `GS1-L03`'s; `location-changes` in `GS1-L12`.
  - **BYTE-FIDELITY PROVEN, NOT ASSERTED.** ~20 official typos/quirks are preserved verbatim by design (`mobilization, of resources`, `Ethics in-human actions`, `irrigation systems storage`, `Geography-Physical`, lowercase `literature`, mixed dash forms). The generated TS module was re-imported and string-compared against the authored JSON — **75/75 exact** — and the non-ASCII inventory (**7× U+2014, 8× U+2019**) cross-checks against three authoring agents' independently reported per-file counts. **Every third-party reproduction checked silently normalised at least one quirk**, which is why none was used as a source; the transcription came from the official PDF only.
  - **SOURCING FACTS WORTH NOT RE-DERIVING.** UPSC publishes **no standalone CSE syllabus document** — `/examinations/revised-syllabus-scheme` holds only Geo-Scientist 2020 and CMS 2018, so the **annual examination notice IS the syllabus of record** (`Notif-CSP-2026-Engl-060226Rev.pdf`, EXAMINATION NOTICE NO 05/2026-CSE, 04.02.2026; Prelims at Section III Part A, Mains pp. 33-35). All 75 lines are **byte-identical across the official 2026, 2025, 2024 and 2022 notices** — unchanged since at least 2022. **The `www.` prefix is MANDATORY** for `sites/default/files` PDFs: the bare `upsc.gov.in` host returns **HTTP 200 with `text/html`** soft-404 content, so the failure **masquerades as success**. And `sites/default/files/GENERAL STUDIES-III.pdf` is **not** the syllabus — it is the Mains **2014 question paper**. Both notices are now registered in `content-sources.yaml` (tier A, `exam: upsc`) so the citation is re-fetchable rather than a bare URL.
  - **Sourcing from the real PDF caught FOUR factual errors in my own agent briefs** — worth remembering as the reason not to reconstruct official text from knowledge: `Disaster and disaster management.` is `GS3-L15`, sitting **before** the five internal-security bullets, not at the end; the syllabus heading prints `Bio diversity` unhyphenated; the official text has lowercase `literature`; and the geophysical-phenomena material is **one** bullet, not two.
  - **GRAIN CONFORMANCE (why weightage/qgen/chapter targeting stays comparable):** max depth **2 in every paper** (the UPPSC tree is max-depth-2 in all 10 papers with zero exceptions), 5-9 depth-1 sections, and a bilingual one-sentence `description` on **every** node — not decorative, since `ingest:embed` embeds `"${title}. ${description}"`; two draft descriptions that described *the syllabus document* rather than the topic were caught and rewritten before they could pollute retrieval. Per-paper rows: `UPSC_PRE_GS1` 33, `UPSC_PRE_CSAT` 13, `UPSC_MAINS_ESSAY` 6, `UPSC_MAINS_GS1` 45, `UPSC_MAINS_GS2` 31, `UPSC_MAINS_GS3` 39, `UPSC_MAINS_GS4` 35 = **202**. Honest deviation reported rather than hidden: Prelims `title.en` median is 16.5 vs UPPSC Prelims' 22.5, because UPSC's text names single-word clauses (`Poverty`, `Inclusion`) where UPPSC's tree merged pairs — padding to hit the median would have drifted titles off official wording.
  - **DB VERIFIED, not assumed:** 202 `upsc` rows; `depth-mismatch=0`, `wrong-parent=0`, `bad-root=0`, and **`d2-reparented-to-root=0`** — the new loader **throws** on an unresolved parent instead of reproducing `syllabus.ts`'s silent `idByPath.get(parentKey) ?? rootId`, which would re-parent an orphan to the paper root with no error. UPPSC's **294 rows untouched**, and **0** UPPSC rows using a `UPSC_` paper code. `meta.source` is `official_syllabus_hand_authored`, deliberately distinct from UPPSC's `official_syllabus` so provenance is legible.
  - **Migration `0112`** binds the 7 papers to their codes in `exams.upsc.paper_structure`, matched on the commission's own `official_label` and **never on array index** — UPSC's Main labels are genuinely offset from the GS numbering (**Paper-II *is* GS-I**), which is why it is explicit rather than arithmetic. This took `usePaperCatalog` from an **empty catalog to 7 papers**; that hook's own docstring had anticipated it exactly (`paper_code: null` ⇒ "no syllabus ingested" ⇒ empty catalog). Paper-A/Paper-B (qualifying language) and Paper-VI/VII (chosen optional, ~25 subjects × 2 papers) stay deliberately **code-less** and excluded from the catalog. `launch_scope_i18n` updated to say the tree IS covered and to name the language/optional papers as out of scope. The migration asserts its own end state (exactly 7 codes, and every code backed by real `syllabus_nodes` rows) with `raise exception`, so a re-seed that changed a label fails loudly.
  - **TWO PREMISE CORRECTIONS, reported rather than papered over.** (1) The brief's step 4 — "map the already-tagged `upsc` question rows (U1's report)" — **had no work in it**: measured live, **5,013 questions, 100% `exam_code='uppsc'`, zero `upsc`**, and zero questions attached to any of the 202 new nodes (consistent with Session 27.5's contamination purge deleting the 399 that once existed). **There is also no "U1" report anywhere in the repo** (`grep "U1\b" docs/ CLAUDE.md` is empty; only U3/U5/U6 are named). A verified no-op, not skipped work. (2) CSAT's "qualifying" flag was **not** encoded onto nodes: it already lives authoritatively in `exams.upsc.paper_structure` as `minimum: {role:"stage_gate", pct:33}` (verified in 0106), and duplicating it would have created a second source of truth.
  - **REAL SAFETY DEFECT FOUND THE HARD WAY: `--write-artifact` implied a PRODUCTION DB WRITE.** Only `--verify-only`/`--dry-run` returned early, so asking for the docs artifact fell through into the upsert path and wrote 202 rows. The write was still verify-gated (the gate had just passed 0 defects) so the data was exactly what had been reviewed — but a docs-refresh flag must never write to a database that is **the same Supabase project for dev and prod**. `--write-artifact` is now **terminal**, with `--write` as the explicit opt-in to do both; the four-mode table is in the CLI header. Re-verified: it now reports "no DB write performed".
  - **Also fixed (regression guard):** `ingest:syllabus`'s `main()` defaulted to iterating ALL of `PAPERS`, so once 7 UPSC papers existed there, a bare `pnpm ingest:syllabus` would try to LLM-structure them and die on `UNAUTHORED` partway through a UPPSC run. Omitting `--exam` now means `DEFAULT_EXAM_CODE` papers only, and says so in its log line.
  - **M23 IS NOW A LIVE GATE, not a hypothetical.** With a UPSC tree in the DB, `classifyPyqId`'s bare `PRE_GS1` plus `resolveSyllabusId`'s missing exam filter would attach UPSC PYQs to **UPPSC's** tree, silently and with no constraint violation. **Fix it as the FIRST step of U5, before the first `ingest:pyq` run.** Today's only guard is the convention "never ingest `upsc_*` files" — which U5 is precisely the act of relaxing. Likewise **M21** (`pyq_ids` validated for existence, not exam) gates a UPSC chapter rollout, and **U6** gates every model-facing path (the mentor already refuses loudly for `upsc` rather than degrading).
  - **Cost discipline:** research, authoring and verification were all done by subagents. One disclosed overrun: an agent testing the `--exam` guard ran `pnpm ingest:syllabus --dry-run`, which is a real billed pipeline, and structured ~3 UPPSC papers (≈3 sonnet calls) before being killed — no DB row touched (`--dry-run` writes only to gitignored `content-raw/parsed/`), and it did confirm the guard, but it was avoidable by reading the code. Two subagent spawns also died on transient API 529s having written nothing; the mechanical merge step was then done in the main loop rather than burning further retries.
  - Verified: `pnpm --filter api typecheck` + `build`, **full `pnpm --filter web build`** (hard rule — `_shared.ts` is a shared module), `pnpm check:paths` (779 files), and `pnpm --filter api prompts:snapshot` → **128 prompts byte-identical** (the 2 WARNs are the documented pre-existing mentor-persona margin, M26). All throwaway scripts deleted. Two untracked `_tmp_u3_audit*` files from the prior U3 session were left alone; every file was staged by explicit name, never `git add -A`.

### Edge-case audit of the UPSC tree, same day (2026-07-30): 4 real fixes, and the `is_live` gate
Four parallel adversarial audits over `e1d9bb6` — hunting for what the commit INTRODUCED or newly EXPOSED, not re-running the happy path. Full actionable index in `docs/OUTSTANDING.md` **§8h**.
  - **HEADLINE — UPPSC IS UNAFFECTED, proven three independent ways rather than argued.** (1) The commit touched **no reader** (`git show --stat`: only `ingest/seed/*`, `ingest/syllabus.ts`, `ingest/_shared.ts`, docs, `0112`), so all read logic is byte-identical code. (2) The new data is provably unreachable from any UPPSC number: **0** paper codes shared across exams, 0 questions on a UPSC paper or node, 0 UPSC notes, 0 UPSC `mv_node_weightage` rows, and 0 UPSC rows in `tests`/`attempts`/`evaluations`/`node_mastery`/`srs_cards`/`discussion_threads`/`current_affairs_items.exam_codes`/`users_profile.target_exam`/`exam_calendar`/`exam_cutoffs`. (3) All 10 UPPSC papers' `topics_count`/`pyq_count`/`notes`/`chapters` were cross-checked against independent exact `count` queries — **10/10 exact match**. No divide-by-zero, NaN or Infinity on any UPSC zero-data path (`treemap.ts` guards `total <= 0` before its divide; `conquest-map.tsx` pre-filters `pyq_count > 0` and renders a real empty state).
  - **FIX 1 (critical) — a valueless value-flag SILENTLY WROTE THE PRODUCTION DATABASE.** `parseArgs` sets a key to boolean `true` when its value is absent or another flag follows it, so `--write-artifact` with the path forgotten — or `--write-artifact --paper X` with the order swapped — collapsed `artifact` to `null`, skipped the terminal artifact guard, and **fell through to the upsert**, re-creating the exact incident that guard had just been added for, from one keystroke. A valueless `--paper` likewise widened a one-paper run to all seven. Value-flags are now rejected before mode resolution and before any DB access; **all three cases verified to refuse.** This is the SECOND time this write-through was found in this one CLI — the durable lesson is that a read-only flag is only safe if a *malformed* invocation of it is also safe.
  - **FIX 2 — the verifier had real gaps, on its own core promise.** Blank verbatim `text` and blank `source` **passed with ZERO defects**: an empty `text` satisfied every other check, still counted as "covered", and still rendered an artifact row — certifying a tree as traceable to official text that traced to nothing. Added `blank_official_line`. Also: byte-identical text on two lines in the SAME paper is now a defect (a duplicated transcription inflates the line/covered totals without adding coverage) while cross-paper identical text is INFO only (two commissions really can print the same phrase). And `verifyCoverage`'s `knownPaperCodes` **default of `[]` silently disabled the `unknown_paper_code` check entirely** — now required, so a future caller cannot drop a check by forgetting an argument (the M24 trap, hit twice before in this repo).
  - **13/13 defect kinds re-proven against the REAL shipped data** (in-memory mutation of the actual 75-line/195-node arrays, not the synthetic set the verifier was originally written against): 20 scenarios, 0 failed to fire. Two claimed design properties independently confirmed — a `dangling_line_reference` does NOT let an invented topic look covered (the real line it should have cited still reports unmapped), and one `missing_parent_path` does NOT cascade into phantom `invented_topic` reports. Artifact `leaves` arithmetic exact for all 7 papers (a childless depth-1 node counts as a leaf — why CSAT reads 9, not 6).
  - **FIX 3 — `addNodeToRevision` was an unguarded M7-class WRITE.** `POST /srs/cards/from-node` took an untrusted `node_id` with no exam check, so a UPPSC user could source a revision card from a UPSC topic — newly reachable only because UPSC nodes now exist. Its own sibling in the same file (`addCurrentAffairsFactToRevision`) had always scoped its source: the same guarded/unguarded asymmetry as the `getTestBoard`/`getMockSeriesBoard` pair the previous audit found. Now `.eq("exam_code", await getUserExam(userId))`, 404 not 403 per convention. The CARD stays exam-agnostic on purpose (0106 §13 — switching exams keeps your deck); it is the SOURCE that is scoped.
  - **FIX 4 — `prove-chapter-retrieval.ts` omitted `filter_exam_code`**, so a verification tool whose header claimed it ran "the EXACT retrieval the mentor runs" was querying across ALL exams (the RPC param defaults to NULL in SQL for API-deploy compatibility). Harmless while uppsc was the only exam with content; the moment a second tree landed it could report a chunk as retrieved that the real exam-scoped mentor would never return — precisely this repo's recorded lesson about evidence that predates a fix. Now derives the exam from the target node. **Re-verified live: rank 1, sim 0.779 — reproducing Session 29's recorded figure exactly, with the filter active.** (Worth noting how that was almost mis-reported: a first run showed "chunk did not appear", which turned out to be my own harness picking `Accountability and Ethical Governance` instead of the `Moral and Political Attitudes` node the question was about — checked before claiming a regression. The intermittent `statement timeout` alongside it is the documented pre-existing slowness, reproducing at the rate the audit measured.)
  - **MITIGATED, not fixed — the loader NEVER deletes, so a RENAMED path leaves a live orphaned subtree** (the old row keeps its `parent_id`, still satisfies every `exam_code='upsc'` read, and keeps its children attached). `verifyCoverage` structurally cannot catch this: it only ever sees the data file, never the DB. Added a paged, **non-destructive** post-write stale-row reconciliation report — deliberately a REPORT rather than a delete, because this repo's cleanup rule forbids a destructive filter that could match rows the run did not itself create, and a node may legitimately be retired by a human. Also now written down rather than left to be discovered: a re-run **overwrites `title_i18n`/`description_i18n`/`meta` wholesale** (`meta` is replaced, not merged) and resets `description_i18n` to NULL where the data file has `null` — **so a reviewer edit to a UPSC node will be clobbered by the next load.** Defensible (the data file is the source of truth by design), but not obvious. `id`/`created_at` are preserved, so `parent_id` re-resolves byte-identically; a second run has genuinely never been exercised (`updated_at > created_at` for 0/202 rows).
  - **⚑ THE `is_live` GATE — flipping `exams.upsc.is_live` is NOT a one-line change, and this is measured, not predicted (`docs/OUTSTANDING.md` §8h U4).** (a) `loadSyllabusCandidates({examCodes: liveExamCodes()})` returns **284 (uppsc only)** today; unscoped it returns **479**, and `is_live` is the ONLY thing excluding the 195 UPSC nodes — with both live, the CA pre-filter stays `enabled=true` and per-exam coverage drops **53% → 31%** of the tree at K=150 (recall was already only 94.2%), **silently, with no log**; if the pre-filter fails open, triage input jumps **+73%** on the repo's highest-frequency LLM call. (b) `caPromptExamCode` **falls back** to `DEFAULT_EXAM_CODE` when more than one exam is live, so triage would be framed as UPPSC while shown a mixed pool — and because it falls back rather than throwing, **U6's `UNAUTHORED` guard does not catch it.** (c) Three hardcoded predicates break silently: `pipeline.ts`'s `paperCode === "PRE_GS1"`, `pickPrelimsMcqNode`'s `p === "PRE_GS1"`, and `pickMainsNode`'s `p.startsWith("MAINS_")` — which `UPSC_MAINS_GS1` **fails**, so a UPSC mains CA question would be silently unmapped while a UPSC prelims CA MCQ would land on **UPPSC's** pool node; `getPrelimsCurrentAffairsNodeId()` also caches a single node id, not per-exam. (d) All 3,688 CA embeddings and 3,194 items are stamped `uppsc`, so a UPSC user would get **zero** CA grounding — and **`ca:embed --all` alone will NOT fix it**: `current_affairs_items.exam_codes` must be recomputed FIRST (that is where the stale value lives, and `caEmbeddingExamCode(['uppsc'])` returns `'uppsc'`), while a fresh `ca:run` cannot self-heal because triage's candidate set is itself gated on `is_live`. The `NULL = shared across all exams` third state 0107 designed for is **completely unexercised — 0 rows repo-wide.**
  - **MY TREE INTRODUCED A TITLE COLLISION, recorded rather than papered over.** Two depth-1 nodes now carry byte-identical titles — `uppsc/PRE_GS1` and `upsc/UPSC_PRE_GS1`, both "Current Events of National and International Importance" — and `ca/prelims-node.ts` finds that node by `ilike` on the TITLE with **no exam filter**, relying solely on the §0 global-paper-code-uniqueness invariant, caching a single id. Safe today (`UPSC_PRE_GS1 !== "PRE_GS1"`), but anyone who "fixes" that predicate by loosening it silently picks one exam's pool node for the other. **Deliberately NOT resolved by renaming my node**: "Current events of national and international importance." is `P1-L01` verbatim official text and both commissions genuinely use the phrase, so the collision is inherent to the domain — the LOOKUP is what should become exam-aware. (Also corrects `docs/multi-exam.md` §1b: that cache is a single node id, not per-paper.)
  - **Confirmed SAFE, recorded so it is not re-investigated:** `ingest:tests` is **question-driven, not `PAPERS`-driven** (it groups published questions by `paper_code`/`year`, and with 0 UPSC questions never iterates a UPSC paper — and would throw on `requireAuthored(...pyqTestTitlePrefix)` if one appeared, i.e. U6 firing loudly); `mv_node_weightage` needs **no refresh** (it is a `GROUP BY` over `questions`, so a zero-question node produces no row — recomputed live and diffed key-for-key: 902/902, 0 missing, 0 stale); `qgen/topup.ts`'s defaulted `examCode` still saves it, double-protected because `LIKE 'PRE_%'`/`'MAINS_%'` match **zero** UPSC codes (the exam prefix anchors before `PRE`); `listFreeNoteNodeIds` is structurally immune (it starts from `notes`, and a paper with zero notes cannot create a bucket — 50 ids, 10 papers × 5, unchanged); `getMasteryMap` returns an honest empty map for a UPSC target rather than crashing; `ingest:verify`/`ingest:embed:verify` are honest for two exams (496 eligible / 496 embedded / 0 missing / 0 orphan, the 404 new rows picked up correctly, nothing spurious flagged); and the pre-existing 40 unmapped published questions are **still exactly 40**, unchanged.
  - **Recorded as a low-severity pre-launch DECISION, not a fix:** `getNodeDetail`/`getPaperTree`/`getPaperTrends` do not exam-check an untrusted id (confirmed live — a UPPSC user reading a UPSC node id gets full detail, while the user-specific parts correctly return `pyq=0/acc=null`). This is the blessed public-reference pattern §0a explicitly declines to gate; **the genuinely new fact is that `upsc.is_live=false`**, so those three now serve an unlaunched exam's syllabus to any authenticated user who guesses an id, while `getSyllabusTree`/`getPaperSummaries` correctly hide it. Disclosure value is near nil (every word is verbatim public government text), but it should be an explicit decision rather than inherited. Also corrected: `usePaperCatalog`'s docblock claim that "every non-UPSC exam yields an EMPTY catalog", which `0112` made false.
  - Verified: `pnpm --filter api typecheck` + `build`, full `pnpm --filter web build`, `pnpm check:paths`, `prompts:snapshot` **128 byte-identical**, and the coverage gate still **0 defects / 75 lines / 195 nodes** after every verifier change. All four audits left the repo untouched (`git status`/`git diff` empty at their end); all throwaway scripts deleted.

## Multi-exam foundation (2026-07-29): `exams` registry + exam-scoped schema — SCHEMA ONLY
Migration `0106_multi_exam_foundation.sql` (commits `616ea9f`, `a47dfe4`). **Nothing became live**: `uppsc` is the sole `is_live` exam; `upsc` and `mppsc` are seeded reference rows with verified paper structures and honest launch-scope copy, carrying **zero** syllabus nodes, questions, chapters and current affairs. Full actionable index in `docs/OUTSTANDING.md` **§8**; deep reference (per-call-site audit + ordered prerequisites) in **`docs/multi-exam.md`**.
  - **THE LOAD-BEARING INVARIANT — read before ingesting a second syllabus.** `syllabus_nodes.paper_code` stays **globally unique across exams**, enforced by RETAINING the pre-existing `syllabus_nodes_paper_path_key (paper_code, path)`. ~30 call sites filter by `paper_code` alone and remain correct *only* because of it, and `ingest:syllabus`'s upsert conflict target IS `(paper_code, path)` — so a shared code would **overwrite the UPPSC tree in place** rather than insert a second one. **A second exam's paper codes MUST be exam-PREFIXED** (`UPSC_PRE_GS1`, never a bare `PRE_GS1`); the prefix also keeps existing `like 'PRE_%'` scans UPPSC-only by construction. Do NOT drop that index — it converts a whole class of silent cross-exam corruption into a loud `23505` at ingest. Proven live in a rolled-back transaction: duplicate bare code → `23505`, exam-prefixed → accepted, unknown exam → `23503` by FK.
  - **`paper_structure` was designed against all three REAL structures at once** (UPSC CSE, UPPSC PCS, MPPSC SSE), each verified against the commission's own notification/exam-plan PDF — never memory or aggregators, which are actively wrong here (many still publish MPPSC's pre-2024 1400/175 figures; several transplant UPSC's 33% CSAT threshold onto MPPSC, whose floor is 40%/30% **by category**). **Five fields exist because a naive shape provably breaks on real data** — do not "simplify" them away: (1) `minimum{role,pct,pct_by_category}` instead of a boolean `is_qualifying`, because the three exams use thresholds for three different purposes — `stage_gate` (CSAT 33%), `evaluation_gate` (UPSC Paper-A/B at 25%: fail and your other papers are **never evaluated**), `merit_floor` (MPPSC 40%/30%, UPPSC General Hindi); (2) `negative_marking` as a **fraction of question marks**, since UPPSC's one-third rule deducts ~0.44 in Prelims Paper I but ~0.67 in Paper II; (3) `optional_group`, since UPSC's Paper-VI/VII must be the SAME chosen subject; (4) `effective_from_year`/`effective_to_year` — UPPSC restructured for Mains 2023, MPPSC for 2026, so without versioning an old PYQ renders against a structure that didn't exist; (5) `question_count: null` meaning **not officially notified** (UPSC fixes marks and duration but never the count — the familiar 100/80 is practice). Contested figures are disclosed per row in `unverified_notes`. **Arithmetic cross-check, validated against the zod contract before commit:** summing only `counts_for_merit` papers plus the interview reproduces each published grand total — UPPSC 1500+100=1600, UPSC **1750**+275=2025 (Paper-A/B qualifying, excluded — this is what caught a first-cut validator that summed all papers to 2350), MPPSC 1500+185=1685.
  - **Columns added only where the exam is NOT derivable, or derivation is too costly for a hot path:** `syllabus_nodes`, `users_profile.target_exam` (distinct from the confusingly-similar `target_exam_year`, a *year*), `tests` (**`paper_code` is nullable — 18 live `daily_quiz` rows prove it**, and this is what makes attempts/analytics/boards derivable), `exam_calendar` (had zero exam identity), `exam_cutoffs`, `embeddings` (an ANN search can't be post-filtered without wrecking recall), `doubt_faq_cache` (a *global* semantic cache would serve one exam's framing to another's user silently, with no model call), `current_affairs_items.exam_codes[]`, `discussion_threads` (nullable = cross-exam). Every other table's decision — DERIVES-EXAM / USER-SCOPED / EXAM-AGNOSTIC / MULTI-EXAM — is recorded with its rationale in the migration's §13. SRS cards are deliberately **exam-agnostic** (a user switching exams should keep their deck). `mv_node_weightage` is already correct — don't "fix" it.
  - **CA items verified MULTI-EXAM (checked, not assumed):** `syllabus_node_ids` is a bare `uuid[]` with no FK and no cardinality constraint — not a scalar, not a join table — so one array can legitimately span several exams' trees with no schema change, and `node_significance` is already a jsonb map keyed BY NODE ID. A national story is relevant to all three and must NOT be duplicated per exam. The added `exam_codes[]` is a **query-path denormalization** (an efficient "CA for my exam" feed), not a second source of truth.
  - **`exam_cutoffs.exam_code` held a PAPER code (`'PRE_GS1'`) and is renamed to `paper_code`.** With a real `exam_code` now on six tables that name was an active trap: `.eq("exam_code","uppsc")` would return zero rows silently, with no error. A real `exam_code` sits beside it.
  - **The four exam-calendar countdowns are now scoped to the user's `target_exam`** (`lib/exam-calendar.ts`) without serialising behind the profile read — a small window of upcoming rows for all exams is fetched in parallel, then narrowed in memory. Before this, a UPPSC aspirant would count down to whichever exam had the nearest date. Returning **no** countdown for an exam with no seeded dates is the correct, honest outcome.
  - **`questions.exam_code` is PROVENANCE ("which exam asked this") and deliberately stays a CHECK, not an FK** — its domain intentionally includes exams we ingest PYQs from but never sell (`up_ro_aro`, `upsssc_pet`, `other`). `0106` extended it to cover `mppsc`, which `0036` omitted. **MEASURED: zero rows are tagged non-`uppsc`** — the Session-27.5 contamination purge deleted the 399 that once existed, so a UPSC rollout starts from zero questions and zero node coverage; there is no overlap corpus to inherit.
  - **EDGE-CASE AUDIT (`a47dfe4`) found two real defects introduced/exposed by the first commit:** (1) **`target_exam` accepted a NON-LIVE exam** — `PATCH /profile {"target_exam":"upsc"}` validated and persisted, stranding the user in an app with 0 nodes and no UI path back (onboarding omits the field; no web surface exposes it). The FK proves an exam EXISTS but cannot express "and it is ready"; reproduced live against a throwaway user, fixed with `lib/exams.ts` `assertSelectableExam` → 400. (2) **`ingest/_shared.ts` REDECLARED `ExamCode`** as a local copy of the shared enum and had already drifted on the very first extension — it never gained `mppsc`, so the ingest pipeline could not classify or label an MPPSC paper, **silently, with no typecheck error** because it was a separate declaration. It now re-exports the shared type, and both `EXAM_PREFIXES` and `classifyPyqId`'s regex derive from `examCodeSchema.options` so the pattern cannot fall behind again (verified every existing id classifies identically, `mppsc` now works, malformed ids still return null). Also fixed my own `istToday()`-called-twice off-by-one, and corrected two comments that asserted a uniqueness guarantee `0106` had moved.
  - **Audited and found CLEAN (recorded so it isn't re-investigated):** zero `export *` name collisions across 983 shared exports (ES modules *silently exclude* an ambiguous name rather than erroring, so this was worth proving); every `tests` upsert targets `onConflict: "slug"`, so the dropped `0078` daily-quiz index (that file was **renumbered `0098` on 2026-07-29** — see the migration-ledger reconciliation entry below) was referenced by no code and there is no `42P10` risk; no code writes `exam_cutoffs`/`exam_calendar` at all; no raw SQL references any dropped index/constraint name; `cutoff-comparison.tsx` never reads `.exam_code`, so that field's changed meaning is rendered nowhere; nothing constructs a `Profile` object literal, so the newly-required `target_exam` cannot throw at parse. **`exams` is readable by the real ANON key** — the documented `42501` trap for a table created outside `db push` did not bite (`0015`'s default privileges cover it) — while anon writes are blocked `42501` and `embeddings`/`doubt_faq_cache` stay closed to anon despite their new columns.
  - **OPS BLOCKER — RESOLVED 2026-07-29, see the migration-ledger reconciliation entry below. (`docs/OUTSTANDING.md` M10 → §0c.) Historical record of the blockage as it stood: `supabase db push` was BLOCKED for this repo.** The remote ledger holds `0079`–`0100` with **no local migration files** (a concurrent branch), so the CLI refuses outright and `--include-all` does not help; `0104`/`0105` are applied but unrecorded. **Do NOT run the `supabase migration repair --status reverted 0079 …` the CLI suggests** — those migrations ARE applied, and marking them reverted makes a later push try to re-run them. `0106` was applied by executing the file in ONE transaction over a direct `pg` connection (temporary devDependency, removed after) and inserting its own ledger row. The real fix is to reconcile the branches. Recorded in [[supabase-headless-migrations]], along with the ordering trap this exposed: a `not null default 'x' references newtable(col)` column is FK-validated for all existing rows at ALTER time, so the parent rows must be seeded BEFORE the first such ALTER, not at the end of the file.
  - Verified: all backfills 100% `uppsc` (294 nodes · 141 profiles · 290 tests · 21,994 embeddings · 2,955 CA items · 40 FAQ-cache rows · 20 cut-offs); seeds parse against the shared zod contract through PostgREST; `getProfile`/`getDashboardSummary`/`getCutoffs`/`computeLearnerProfile` driven live post-migration; full production build (api typecheck + build, web build, lint exit 0 with no new warnings, `check:paths`) clean. **NOT done, deliberately:** the `NEEDS-EXAM-FILTER` reads, the `match_embeddings`/`match_doubt_faq` RPC filters, and the notes-duplication decision — all catalogued in `docs/OUTSTANDING.md` §8 rather than half-implemented.

## Multi-exam M1-M4 closed (2026-07-29): the four blocking prerequisites
Migrations `0107_multi_exam_retrieval.sql` + `0108_drop_redundant_embeddings_index.sql`, plus the call-site work `docs/multi-exam.md` §1 catalogued. `uppsc` is still the only `is_live` exam and the other two still carry zero content — what changed is that ingesting a second exam's content is no longer unsafe. Full per-site detail in `docs/multi-exam.md`; actionable index in `docs/OUTSTANDING.md` §8a.
  - **M1 — `ingest:syllabus` can load a second exam.** `PaperDef` carries `exam`; `upsertNode` stamps `exam_code`. **The conflict target stays `(paper_code, path)` deliberately** — adding exam to the key would let two exams share a bare code, which ~30 paper_code-only reads elsewhere would then silently mix. New `assertPaperCodeScoped(exam, paperCode)` throws BEFORE any model spend or write unless a non-default exam's codes are exam-prefixed, so §0 is enforced in code, not by convention. `--exam` added.
  - **M2 — the unscoped `syllabus_nodes` reads.** `getSyllabusTree`/`getPaperSummaries` take a **required** `examCode` (a defaulted one would let a caller silently keep the bug — the same reasoning is used throughout this change). `dashboard.ts` resolves the exam once and shares it between countdown and weakness radar; `learner-profile.ts` reuses the `target_exam` it already reads; `profile-analytics.ts`, `mastery/compute.ts`, `qgen/topup.ts`, `ingest/verify.ts` scoped. New `lib/exams.ts`: `getUserExam` / `examCodeForNode` / `liveExamCodes`.
    - **TWO CORRECTIONS to the original audit, found by implementing it.** (1) `getMasteryMap`'s existing `exam?` arg is NOT the one to wire — it is the PROVENANCE filter on `questions.exam_code` (the UI's "UPPSC-asked only" toggle, affecting tile weight) and was already in use; tree scoping needed a separate `targetExam`. Do not merge them. (2) `dashboard.ts:319`'s `paper_code::path` key does NOT collide across exams — paper codes are globally unique — but the read was **unranged**, so it silently truncates at PostgREST's 1000-row cap as exams are added, dropping ancestors and whole radar sections. Both it and `learner-profile.ts:50` are now scoped **and** paged.
    - **Two sites deliberately NOT single-exam-filtered, with reasons recorded in-file:** `ca/syllabus-candidates.ts` is multi-exam BY DESIGN (0106 §11 keeps one national CA item mapped across exams precisely so it isn't duplicated and re-triaged per exam — a hard filter would re-pay the two most expensive LLM calls per copy); it now takes the **live** exam set instead, so a reference/half-ingested exam never pollutes the triage prompt. `ingest/embed-coverage.ts` is a global report and scoping it would under-report.
  - **M3 — `match_embeddings` filters on exam INSIDE the RPC** (0107 §2). `EmbeddingRow.exam_code` is a **required** field and `retrieveGrounding`/`retrieveContext` take a **required** `examCode`, so the compiler forced every writer and all ~12 retrieval call sites to name their exam. Writers derive it from the NODE — never from `questions.exam_code`, which is provenance and may name an exam nobody can select (`up_ro_aro`, `upsssc_pet`). `embeddings.exam_code` became **nullable = shared across all exams**, the honest third state for a CA item mapped into several exams' trees (one embedding row per item per locale leaves no room for a per-exam copy); `ca/embed-exam.ts` is the shared single-exam→stamp / multi-exam→NULL rule. **No re-embed was needed** — every existing row is genuinely `uppsc`.
  - **M4 — `match_doubt_faq` filters on exam** (0107 §3). Because `upsertFaqCache` reuses that same lookup as its near-duplicate check, one change scopes the cache in BOTH directions: a user is never served another exam's framing, and one exam's regeneration can never overwrite another exam's row. `cache-health.ts`'s boot probe now passes the new argument, so a stale pre-0107 RPC is reported at startup instead of via a user complaint.
  - **WHY THE RPC PARAMS DEFAULT TO NULL rather than being required.** A required SQL parameter couples the migration and the API deploy: until the new API rolls out, every old-code call fails — and both retrieval paths degrade gracefully on an RPC error (they answer ungrounded), so the failure would be **silent quality loss**, the worst kind. The SQL stays backward-compatible; the enforcement lives in TypeScript, where a missing `examCode` is a compile error.
  - **VERIFIED BY SEEDING A REAL SECOND EXAM, not by checking uppsc still works** — a throwaway `upsc` tree (`UPSC_PRE_GS1`), its own embeddings, a shared NULL-exam chunk, a per-exam cached FAQ answer, and a throwaway user whose `target_exam` is `upsc`: **32/32 assertions, isolation checked in BOTH directions.** The assertion worth keeping: with two near-identical chapters on one topic, one per exam, the *other* exam owned the ENTIRE unfiltered top-2 — a post-filter would have left that user with **zero** grounding, while the in-RPC filter returned a full top-2. That is the concrete reason the filter cannot live in TypeScript. Every synthetic row deleted by an id captured at insert time; `exams.upsc.is_live` restored.
  - **MEASURED, and a correction to an easy misreading:** `match_embeddings` on this DB (22,148 rows) intermittently exceeds Supabase's statement timeout — but **the exam filter is not the cause**. Unfiltered (`filter_exam_code := null`, i.e. exactly the pre-0107 behaviour) took ~7-8s and often timed out; `'uppsc'` ~5.8s; `'upsc'` (a small partition) ~0.2s. The filter is neutral-to-helpful. This is the pre-existing slowness already noted elsewhere in this file — don't re-diagnose it as a regression from this change.
  - **MISTAKE MADE AND CORRECTED:** 0107 created `embeddings_exam_locale_source_idx`, column-for-column identical to the `embeddings_exam_locale_idx` 0106 had already created — pure write amplification on the hottest-inserted table. Dropped by `0108` as a separate append-only migration rather than by editing an applied file, since this repo has been burned once by the ledger and the files disagreeing (`docs/OUTSTANDING.md` §0c). Check for an existing index before adding one.
  - **Partial M8 fell out of M3:** `ca/pipeline.ts` now writes `current_affairs_items.exam_codes` from the nodes triage actually chose instead of the `{uppsc}` default — required, because the item's embedding is stamped from that same set and the two must not disagree. `gs_papers`/`is_up_specific` stay UPPSC-shaped. **[CORRECTED 2026-08-01 — this was only ever true of the ARCHIVE insert.** The published/draft insert beside it never carried an `exam_codes` key at all, so every user-visible row silently took the column default `array['uppsc']` (0106) from this commit until the fix below. See "CA exam-scope write pair" — the two paths are now structurally unable to diverge.**]**
  - **STILL OPEN, deliberately:** M5-M9 (§8b) — in particular M7 (`community.ts:91`, `on-demand.ts:154`, `tests.ts:259` accept untrusted node ids and assert only a shared `paper_code`) and the daily-quiz trio (M6). Verified: `pnpm --filter api typecheck`/`build`, `pnpm --filter web build`, `oxlint` (no new warnings), `pnpm check:paths` all clean; `supabase db push` applied 0107 + 0108 to the cloud DB via the direct IPv6 host.

### Edge-case audit, same day: three real bugs, one of them live and user-visible
Adversarial pass over the M1-M4 commit — failure paths and live measurement, not a re-run of the happy path. Detail in `docs/multi-exam.md` §3f (renumbered from §3d on 2026-07-29 — that file had two sections numbered 3d).
  - **REAL, LIVE, USER-VISIBLE (pre-existing, found inside a function this change already owned): the /learn papers grid's "M PYQs" badge was truncated to 1,000 rows.** `getPaperSummaries`' question-count select was unranged over **4,252** matching rows, so PostgREST returned 1,000 and the badge under-reported by ~76% — **PRE_GS1 showed 110 instead of 1,003**, PRE_CSAT 193 instead of 870. It pre-dates the multi-exam work (it broke when the PYQ bank tripled in Session 27.5) and had gone unnoticed because a truncated select looks exactly like a small result set. Paged; verified per paper against exact counts — all 10 now match.
  - **A required parameter that TypeScript could not actually require.** `getMasteryMap`'s `targetExam` sat after two optional params, so it could only be `targetExam?: string` — i.e. precisely the silently-defaults-to-UPPSC shape M2 exists to eliminate. Converted to an options object (`getMasteryMap(userId, { paperCode?, exam?, targetExam })`) so the compiler demands it. **Worth remembering: "make it required" is not achievable by adding a trailing parameter — check the signature actually forces the decision.**
  - **A latency regression I introduced, caught by reading my own diff:** `dashboard.ts` resolved the user's exam serially before its fan-out, adding a round trip to the app's hottest endpoint for one short column. Now fetched in parallel with the day's progress.
  - **Two latent twins of the bug above, fixed pre-emptively:** `getGradedAnswers` and `recomputeMastery` read unranged. Measured: the heaviest real account has 359 graded answers, so neither truncates *today* — but a handful of full 150-question mocks crosses the cap, and a truncated read there silently drops answers from the weakness radar, the papers grid's accuracy and the mentor's learner profile at once, and silently DOWNGRADES mastery levels. Both paged and `.in()`-chunked.
  - **RECORDED, NOT FIXED — the one caveat to "no re-embed was needed":** all 3,588 existing current-affairs embeddings are stamped `uppsc` from 0106's backfill, and `caEmbeddingExamCode` only applies to NEW writes. That is correct today (every CA item is UPPSC-only), but before a second exam goes live, existing CA items need `exam_codes` recomputed plus a `ca:embed --all`, or genuinely national items stay invisible to it. Syllabus/question/note chunks need nothing.
  - **Also recorded:** 40 published questions have no syllabus node, so nothing derives their exam and their embeddings take the default (`ingest:embed` now warns with the count instead of being silent — see §8d M19); and `getScoreTrajectory` renders a raw paper code for an attempt on another exam's paper, which is unreachable today (no exam-switching UI, M13) and better than silently dropping the attempt.
  - **Confirmed NOT bugs, so they aren't re-investigated:** both RPC drops took effect — a 5-arg `match_embeddings` / 3-arg `match_doubt_faq` call resolves and executes rather than returning PostgREST's `PGRST203` "could not choose the best candidate function", which is what a leftover overload would produce; the `questions→syllabus_nodes(exam_code)` and `notes→syllabus_nodes(exam_code)` embeds resolve unambiguously against live data; `entitlements.listFreeNoteNodeIds` is already per-exam (it ranks top-5 *per paper_code*, which §0 makes globally unique); `syllabus_nodes` has 0 NULL `exam_code` rows.
  - **CONCURRENT PROCESS — did not touch it.** Mid-audit another process began adding `exam_code`/`rubric_kind` to `evaluationSchema` + `services/evaluation/rubric.ts` (that is M5 work) and left the tree with one typecheck error in `evaluate.ts`'s `mapEvaluation`, a function this change never touches. Proved the error was theirs rather than assuming: `evaluate.ts` has zero working-tree changes from this session, and a typecheck in an isolated **git worktree** pinned to this session's own commit — with `@neev/shared` re-pointed at that commit's copy so their in-flight edit could not leak in through the workspace symlink — exits 0. Staged only this session's files by explicit name, never `git add -A`.
  - Re-verified after the fixes: **13/13** against a freshly seeded second exam (tree/papers/mastery isolation, plus a real end-to-end run of the dashboard, learner profile and profile analytics for both a UPSC user and a real UPPSC account with history). Harness gotcha worth remembering: `getDashboardSummary` and friends read `currentUserId()`, so a service-level probe must wrap them in `runWithUser(...)` or they throw "called outside an authenticated context" — that is the harness's bug, not the product's. All synthetic rows deleted by captured id; `exams.upsc.is_live` restored.

## Multi-exam go-live set (2026-07-29): M5-M9 closed — boards, daily quiz, node validation, CA, community
Migrations `0109_evaluation_exam_scope.sql` + `0110_discussion_threads_exam_backfill.sql`. `uppsc` remains the sole `is_live` exam and `upsc`/`mppsc` still carry zero content — what changed is that a second exam's users could now be shown their own data without silently mixing into UPPSC's. Actionable index: `docs/OUTSTANDING.md` §8b (all five now ✅); deep reference: `docs/multi-exam.md` §3a/§3d/§3e.
  - **VERIFIED AGAINST A REAL SECOND EXAM, not "uppsc still works": 53/53 assertions** against the live cloud DB with a throwaway `upsc` tree (`UPSC_PRE_GS1`), its own published question bank, one throwaway user per exam, real evaluations (GS + essay), a real second-exam daily quiz built end to end, real threads and a real CA item — isolation checked in BOTH directions. Every synthetic row deleted by an id captured at insert time (per the cleanup rule), `exams.upsc.is_live` restored, and a post-run audit confirmed **0 non-`uppsc` rows** left in `syllabus_nodes`/`tests`/`evaluations`/`questions`/`current_affairs_items`/`users_profile`/`discussion_threads`.
  - **M5 — the rubric registry is now exam-aware, which is what the board fix forced.** `mv_mains_weekly_board` pooled every exam's answer writing into one ranking; the subtler half was that 0069, `services/scoreboard.ts` and `getEvaluationPercentile` all split Essay from GS with the LITERAL comparison `rubric_version <> 'essay-v1'`. That string is *UPPSC's* essay rubric — a second exam's (UPSC's essay is ~1000-1200 words at 125 marks, a genuinely different scheme) satisfies `<>` and would have been swept into the GS board, the exact mixing 0069 exists to prevent. Encoding a segmentation axis as a literal version string does not survive a second exam.
